24	ARE HAZARDOUS AREAS CLEARLY MARKED?
25	ARE HANDRAILS IN PLACE AROUND THE RIG FLOOR, MUD PITS, AND STAIRWAYS?

3.10.2	FRACTURING OPERATIONS
1	EMERGENCY SHUT IN PRESSURE TESTED
2	TANKS WITH BIOCIDE
3	ALL EQUIPMENT FLUSHED WITH FILTERED WATER
4	TREE SAVER INSTALLED AND SAVED
5	ENSURE THAT X'TREE VALVES ARE PROPERLY FUNCTIONING AFTER FRAC JOB
6	FLUID FILTERED

3.10.3	WELL TESTING
1	REMOTE SHUT IN PRESSURE VALVE IN PLACE & FUNCTION TESTED
2	ANNULUS PRESSURE GAUGE IN PLACE
3	RELIEF PRESSURE SYSTEM IN PLACE IN SEPARATOR, DESANDER AND ANY OTHER PRESSURE VESSELS
4	CORRECT RIG UP: PIPE ANCHORED, CORRECT DISTANCE
5	PRESSURE SIGNS/BARRIER TAPE
6	PIPING/MANIFOLD/TRIPHASIC/DESANDER/HEATER CERTIFICATION

3.10.4	WIRELINE
1	CONDITION OF BOP/LAST INSPECTION
2	ALL EQUIPMENT GROUNDED AND CONNECTED TO UNIT SOURCES PROPERLY CHAINED
3	CONDITION OF LINES
4	PCE & LUBRICATOR BODY TEST
5	GREASE WORKING PROPERLY
6	PRESSURE EQUIPMENT CERTIFICATION PAPERS, HISTORY CARDS, MAINTENANCE RECORDS REVIEWED
7	NOTIFICATION TO BASE RADIO ON PERFORATING OPERATIONS
8	NOTIFICATION TO OTHER ACTIVITIES BESIDE PAD
9	CASING TO RIG VOLTAGE MONITOR INSTALLED AND CHECKED FREQUENTLY
10	SAFETY GROUNDING STRAPS CORRECTLY INSTALLED AND CONNECTED TO UNIT
11	ALL RADIO TRANSMITTERS TURNED OFF AND WARNING SIGNS TESTED
12	GENERATOR OFF OR MAIN DISCONNECT SWITCH MUST BE OFF
13	LINE OF FIRE CLEARED OF ALL PERSONNEL
14	ONLY ENGINEER ARMS PERFORATING GUNS
15	SAFETY SWITCH AND POWER RESTORED ONLY AFTER GUNS IS 100 FT BELOW GROUND
16	POWER SHUT DOWN COMING OUT OF THE HOLE AT 100 FT BELOW GROUND
17	ONLY ENGINEER DISARMS PERFORATING GUNS WHEN NECESSARY
18	AREA POLICED AND ALL WASTE EXPLOSIVES PLACED IN REMNANTS BOX
19	PROPER TRUCK SPOTTING UPWIND
20	HOLE COVERED BEFORE ANY EQUIPMENT TRANSPORTED TO WELL HEAD
21	SHEAVES CHAIN SECURE TO SURFACE CASING OR WELL HEAD
22	CHECK CABLE DATA CARD DATE
23	WINCH SHUTDOWN WORKING PROPERLY

3.11	RIG ACCOMMODATION

3.11.1	ACCOMMODATION ELECTRIC REHEATERS
1	TURN THERMOSTAT ON.
2	CHECK OPERATION OF AIR FLOW SWITCH BY CLOSING/OPENING THE RESPECTIVE FIRE DAMPER TO THE AREA UNDER TEST.

3.11.2	BOILER CIRCULATION PUMP
1	RUN PUMP FOR A SHORT PERIOD AND CHECK FOR LEAKS, VIBRATION.
2	CHECK MAXIMUM DISCHARGE PRESSURE. RECORD AS FOUND.
3	CHECK ACTUAL MOTOR LOAD AMPERES. RECORD AS FOUND (CONSULT ELECTRICIAN).

3.11.3	AIR CONDITIONER
1	CHECK FOR CLEANLINESS OF FILTERS.
2	INSPECT UNIT FOR CORROSION, DAMAGE AND LEAKS.
3	LISTEN FOR UNUSUAL NOISE AND/OR VIBRATION.

3.11.4	LIGHTING HAZARDOUS AREAS
1	VISUALLY CHECK LIGHTS FOR LOOSE OR BROKEN SOCKETS, INSECURE OR BROKEN MOUNTS, CORRODED, WATERTIGHT AND FLAME PROOF INTEGRITY OF JUNCTION BOX, LOOSE ELECTRICAL CONNECTIONS, DAMAGED REFLECTORS, BROKEN OR CRACKED GLASSWARE AND CORRODED OR RUSTED CONDUIT FITTINGS.

3.11.5	WATER TREATMENT
1	CHECK OPERATION OF WATER TREATMENT SYSTEM (CHLORINATOR OR ULTRAVIOLET STERILIZER).
2	CHECK WATER ANALYSIS REPORT.

3.11.6	POTABLE WATER SYSTEM
1	VISUALLY INSPECT TANK - CHECK FOR CORROSION ETC.
2	UT WELD SEAMS EXTERNALLY.
3	PERFORM WALL THICKNESS CHECK WITH UT SET.
4	HAVE RELIEF VALVES TESTED/RE-SET BY A QUALIFIED INSPECTOR.

3.11.7	PUMP
1	WITH SYSTEM IN OPERATION, CHECK ALL VALVE GLANDS FOR LEAKS.
2	CHECK MAXIMUM DISCHARGE PRESSURE AND RECORD.
3	CHECK ACTUAL LOAD ON MOTOR AMPS AND RECORD.
4	ENSURE COUPLING GUARDS ARE IN PLACE.

3.11.8	SEWAGE SYSTEM
1	FILL SURGE TANK WITH WATER TO CHECK HIGH LEVEL ALARM. ENSURE DRAIN VALVE IS CLOSED.
2	TURN ON MACERATOR TO ESTABLISH FLOW THROUGH UNIT AND BACKFLUSH SYSTEM.
3	RUN UNIT FOR ONE HOUR TREATING SEWAGE. CHECK THAT UNIT OPERATES SATISFACTORILY.

3.11.9	LIVING QUARTERS
1	CLEAN & ORDERLY
2	PROPERLY GROUNDED (DATE EARTHING LAST MEASURED & VERIFIED)
3	ELECTRICAL WIRING & OUTLETS IN GOOD ORDER (DATE OF LAST SUPPLY VOLTAGE CHECK)
4	TWO MEANS OF EGRESS PROVIDED, AND CLEARLY MARKED
5	SMOKE DETECTOR PROVIDED (DATE OF LAST FUNCTION)
6	FIRE EXTINGUISHER / HOSE CABINETS PROVIDED
7	ALARMS AUDIBLE IN ALL AREAS (DATE OF LAST FUNCTION TEST)
8	VENTILATION, HEATING / AIR CONDITIONING IN SATISFACTORY WORKING ORDER
9	EMERGENCY LIGHTING (DATE OF LAST TEST)
10	MUSTER POINT / SAFE AREAS CLEARLY MARKED (ALL PERSONNEL BRIEFED & KNOW THE DRILL)
11	ADEQUATE SEPTIC TANKS

3.11.10	WASHING MACHINE
1	RUN MACHINE AND CHECK FOR LEAKS AT DOOR GASKET OR HOSE CONNECTIONS. CHECK FOR UNUSUAL NOISE AND VIBRATION.

3.11.11	CLOTHES DRYER
1	START UP THE UNIT AND TEST THE INTERLOCK DOOR SWITCH AND THE HEATING ELEMENTS FOR PROPER OPERATION.

3.11.12	DUMB WAITER
1	CHECK CLUTCH FOR PROPER OPERATION. UNIT SHOULD LIFT NO MORE THAN 10% ABOVE RATED CAPACITY.
2	CHECK BRAKE AND LIMIT SWITCH OPERATION. CAB SHOULD STOP WITHIN 1/2" OF LANDING.
3	CYCLE UNIT SEVERAL TIMES UP AND DOWN TO ENSURE PROPER OPERATION.
4	CHECK DOOR SWITCHES OR INTERLOCKS TO ENSURE UNIT DOES NOT OPERATE IF DOORS ARE OPEN.

3.11.13	GALLEY REFRIGERATOR
1	RECORD MOTOR AMPS.
2	CHECK THERMOSTAT CONTROL FOR PROPER SETTING AND OPERATION. RECORD TEMPERATURE.

3.11.14	WALK-IN-FREEZER / CHILLER
1	CHECK LOW-PRESSURE SWITCH WHILE UNIT IS OPERATING: SHUT THE MAIN LIQUID LINE VALVE AND RECORD SUCTION PRESSURE AT WHICH THE UNIT STOPS; OPEN VALVE AND RECORD PRESSURE AT WHICH IT STARTS.
2	CHECK HIGH-PRESSURE SWITCH: SLOWLY CLOSE CONDENSER OUTLET VALVE AND RECORD PRESSURE AT WHICH THE UNIT STOPS; OPEN AND RECORD START PRESSURE.
3	CHECK HEAD PRESSURE/TEMPERATURE WHEN COMPRESSOR RUNNING AND COMPARE WITH PREVIOUS MONTHS READINGS.
4	CHECK WALK-IN FREEZER AND CHILL ROOM DOORS FOR PROPER SEALING AND DOOR LOCKING.
5	CHECK REMOTE TEMPERATURE GAUGES AND PRESSURE GAUGES FOR ACCURACY AND PHYSICAL DAMAGE.

3.11.15	MESSHALL REFRIGERATOR
1	CHECK THERMOSTAT CONTROL FOR PROPER SETTING AND OPERATION. RECORD TEMPERATURE.
2	CHECK REFRIGERATOR DOOR FOR PROPER SEALING AND DOOR LATCH MECHANISM.

3.11.16	HOSPITAL REFRIGERATOR
1	CHECK THERMOSTAT CONTROL FOR PROPER SETTING AND OPERATION. RECORD TEMPERATURE.
2	CHECK REFRIGERATOR DOOR FOR PROPER SEALING AND DOOR LATCH MECHANISM.

3.11.17	QUARTER-FITTINGS
1	CARRY OUT A VISUAL INSPECTION OF QUARTER-FITTINGS - COMMENT ON FINDINGS.

3.12	WORKSHOPS / WAREHOUSES / OFFICES

3.12.1	ELECTRICAL TOOLS (PORTABLE)
1	CHECK PM RECORDS FOR MAINTENANCE BEING CARRIED OUT.
2	ENSURE THAT NO PORTABLE ELECTRICAL TOOLS ARE FITTED WITH POSITIVE LOCKING DEVICES.

3.12.2	TORQUE WRENCH
1	VERIFY FROM PM RECORDS THAT MAINTENANCE ON THIS EQUIPMENT IS UP TO DATE.

3.12.3	WELDING MACHINES
1	INSPECT TERMINAL CONNECTIONS AND WIRING FOR DEFECTS AND DETERIORATION.
2	MEGGER TEST DC GENERATOR/RECTIFIER AND RECORD.
3	TEST RUN WELDING MACHINE AND CHECK FOR EXCESSIVE BEARING NOISE.
4	VERIFY AMPERAGE OUTPUT OF MACHINE AGREES WITH CONTROL SETTING ON MACHINE.
5	ENSURE THAT ALL WELDING IS BEING DONE USING BOTH WORK AND RETURN WIRES.

3.12.4	WELDER'S TOOLS
1	RIGID THREADING MACHINE - CHECK OPERATION OF OIL SYSTEM, ENSURE JAW INSERT DIES AND CUTTING DIES ARE IN GOOD CONDITION.
2	POWER HACKSAW - CHECK ALL FASTENERS FOR SECURITY, ENSURE ALL GUARDS ARE IN PLACE. CHECK CONDITION AND TENSION OF CUTTING BLADE.
3	PEDESTAL GRINDER - ENSURE ALL WHEEL GUARDS ARE IN PLACE. INSPECT WHEELS FOR WEAR/DAMAGE; ENSURE GOGGLES PROVIDED.
4	ANGLE GRINDERS - VISUALLY CHECK FOR DAMAGE TO GRINDER AND CABLE; ENSURE DISCS ARE IN GOOD CONDITION AND GUARDS IN PLACE.
5	ELECTRICAL WELDING EQUIPMENT - ENSURE ALL COMPONENTS ARE IN GOOD CONDITION: ELECTRODE HOLDER, LEADS, CONNECTORS, MASKS, GLOVES.
6	GAS WELDING EQUIPMENT - ENSURE ALL COMPONENTS ARE IN GOOD CONDITION: NOZZLES, TORCHES, HOSES (CHECK FOR CORRECT CRIMPS), GAUGES, GLOVES.
7	SIGNS - CHECK THAT ALL EQUIPMENT WARNING AND INSTRUCTION SIGNS ARE IN PLACE AND CLEARLY LEGIBLE.
8	FIRE EXTINGUISHERS AND FIRE BLANKETS - CHECK THAT THEY ARE IN PLACE AND NOT OBSTRUCTED.
9	ENSURE GOOD HOUSEKEEPING.

3.12.5	THIRD PARTY EQUIPMENT
1	CHECK RECORDS TO ENSURE THAT INSPECTION OF THIRD PARTY EQUIPMENT IS TAKING PLACE.
2	ENSURE THAT AN UP TO DATE ANNUAL INSPECTION CERTIFICATE IS AVAILABLE FOR ALL THIRD PARTY EQUIPMENT.
3	ENSURE THAT ALL THIRD PARTY EQUIPMENT IS INSTALLED CORRECTLY AS PER RECOGNISED INSTALLATION STANDARDS.

3.12.6	RADIO EQUIPMENT
1	MAKE VISUAL INSPECTION OF RADIOS. CHECK FOR EXCESSIVE HEATING.
2	CHECK EXTERNAL ANTENNA INSTALLATIONS; ENSURE INSTALLATION STANDARDS ARE BEING MAINTAINED ON ALL COMMUNICATION EQUIPMENT.

3.13	WELLHEAD / CHRISTMAS TREE

1	CELLAR COVERED
2	VALVES IN GOOD WORKING CONDITIONS
3	ANNULAR PRESSURE GAUGE IN PLACE
4	LADDER AND HAND RAILS SECURED
5	TREE CAP O-RING CHECKED
6	PRESSURE GAUGES TEFLON TAPED
7	TREE PROPERLY DRESSED OUT

API:		American Petroleum Institute
BHA:		Bottom Hole Assembly
BHP:		Bottom Hole Pressure
BHST:		Bottom Hole Static Temperature
BHT:		Bottom Hole Temperature

BSR:		Bending Strength Ratio
CCI:		Cutting Carrying Index
COSC:		Combined Operation Safety Case
CRS:		Critical Rotary Speed
CT:		Coiled Tubing
CTSI:		Coiled Tubing Sensor Interface
DC:		Drill Collar
DHSV:		Down Hole Safety Valve
DP:		Drill Pipe
DST:		Drill Stem Test
ECD:		Equivalent Circulating Density
ESD:		Emergency Shut Down
FIT:		Formation Integrity Test
FLOT:		Formation Leak Off Test
GPM:		Gallons Per Minute
HO:		Hole Opener
HTHP:		High Temperature High Pressure
HWDP:		Heavy Weight Drill Pipe
IADC:		International Association of Drilling Contractors
ID:		Inner Diameter
JSA:		Job Safety Analysis
LAS:		Liquid Additive System
LCM:		Loss Circulation Material
LMRP:		Low Marine Riser Preventer
LWD:		Logging While Drilling
MAASP:		Maximum Allowable Annular Surface Pressure
MD:		Measured Depth
MGS:		Mud Gas Separator
MSDS:		Material Safety Data Sheet
MWD:		Measuring While Drilling
NB:		Near Bit
NMDC:		Non Magnetic Drill Collar
OBM:		Oil Based Mud
OD:		Outer Diameter
OIM:		Offshore Installation Manager
PDM:		Positive Displacement Motor
PGB:		Permanent Guide Base
PNP:		Propylene Glycol Normal Propyl Ether
POOH:		Pull Out Of Hole
PPE:		Personal Protective Equipment
PVT:		Pit Volume Totalizer
QA:		Quality Assurance
QC:		Quality Control
R & R:		Running & Retrieval
RIH:		Run In Hole
RKB:		Rotary Kelly Bushing
ROP:		Rate Of Penetration
ROV:		Remote Operating Vehicle
RPM:		Revolutions Per Minute
SCR:		Silicon Controlled Rectifier
SIDPP:		Shut In Drill Pipe Pressure
SIMOP:		Simultaneous Operations
SIWP:		Shut In Wellhead Pressure
SSTT:		Sub Surface Test Tree
TD:		Total Depth
TFM:		Tubing Forces Module
THOT:		Tubing Hanger Orienting Tool
THRT:		Tubing Hanger Running Tool
TOC:		Top Of Cement
TT:		Thickening Time
WSS:		Well Site Supervisor
